Findings\n\n### REQUIRED\n\n#### R-1 ÔÇö a partially unreadable wallet is reported as a measured \"did not pay\", and the non-breach exclusion is printed from it\n\n**Where.** `_paid` `tools:355-377` (the `readable` counter at `tools:374` and the\n`return False if readable else None` at `tools:377`); `_attribute` `tools:380-412` (the readings at\n`tools:393`, the own-funds text at `tools:400-406`).\n\n**What happens.** `_balances` reads a wallet through **two different endpoints** and turns a failure of\neither into `None` (`tools:331-336` for `user_state`, `tools:341-345` for `spot_user_state`). `_paid`\ncounts a key as `readable` whenever that one key parses on both sides. So a wallet counts as measured\nas soon as **either** endpoint answers ÔÇö and the verdict `False` is rendered by `_attribute` as the flat\nwords **\"did not pay\"**, with no unreadable marker anywhere in the sentence.\n\n**Why that matters here specifically.** On the account this probe runs against, one of those two keys is\na decoy. The candidate's own comment says so:\n\n> `# A unifiedAccount keeps its USDC in the spot balance (perp accountValue reads 0.0 ÔÇö r1 on testnet);` ÔÇö `tools:486`\n\nEvery dollar the master holds is in `usdc_total`; `accountValue` reads `0.0` before and after, always.\nThat permanently-readable `0.0` is enough to set `readable = 1`. Consequence: **for the master wallet on\nthis account, the `UNREADABLE` path the round-2 repair added can only fire if BOTH endpoints fail at\nonce.** A single transient failure of `spot_user_state` ÔÇö the endpoint that carries the only balance\ncapable of paying ÔÇö is reported as a measurement that it did not make.\n\n**Demonstrated** (`C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH/mutroot3/tests/test_reviewer_partial3.py`, run against the\n**unmodified** candidate, fake venue objects only, `3 passed`):\n\n```\nRESULT : DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED\nFINDING: spotSend was NOT refused and the AGENT wallet's own balance DECREASED while the account's\n         readable balances did not: the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach)\n         [account did not pay, agent wallet paid]\nMASTER READINGS: {'before': {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': '983.987457'},\n                  'after':  {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': None}}\n```\n\nThe record says the account **did not pay**. The account's only funded balance was **never read**. The\nthird probe in that file shows the same output for a run in which the master really did pay 6 USDC ÔÇö the\ndecrease is simply invisible.\n\n**This is deliberate, not an oversight**, which is why it needs a decision rather than a patch: the\nshipped unit test pins the partial case at `tests:677-684` ÔÇö\n\n```python\nassert paid({\"accountValue\": None, \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"},\n            {\"accountValue\": \"5.0\",  \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"}, 1.0) is False\n```\n\nÔÇö under a test named `ÔÇĞ_unreadable_honest`. The parametrized case that does exercise unreadability\n(`tests:595-598`) blanks **both** endpoints, so the partial case has no coverage at all.\n\n**Sub-case R-1(b): the exclusion survives even when the sentence does say UNREADABLE.** With the master\nfully unreadable, the finding still reads\n`ÔÇĞ the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach) [account UNREADABLE, agent wallet paid]`\n(`test_DEMO_A`, ┬ğ4e file). `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` (`tools:403`) is an exclusion claim; it requires a\ncomplete, readable master measurement, and in this branch there is none. The clause\n`\"while the account's readable balances did not\"` (`tools:402-403`) is vacuously true when there are no\nreadable balances, and is being used to carry the conclusion.\n\n**Why REQUIRED and not a NIT.** This is round-2's R-1(b) surviving in the case that matters. Round 2\nasked that an unreadable side be named unreadable and never treated as measured; the repair implemented\nthat only for the all-or-nothing failure, and on this account the all-or-nothing failure is the one shape\nthat essentially cannot occur, because a structurally-zero decoy balance always votes \"readable\". The\nerror direction is the flattering one ÔÇö the tool tells the owner the master did not pay, about money it\ndid not look at ÔÇö and the sentence it prints is this package's deliverable.\n\n**Remedy (small, local, failing inputs already written).** Two lines of intent:\n1. `_paid` should distinguish *measured-and-unchanged* from *not measured*: treat a wallet as measured\n   only when every key parsed on both sides (or, minimally, return `None` when any key was unreadable and\n   no readable key decreased). `tests:677-684` then needs its expectation flipped from `False` to `None`.\n2. `_attribute` must not print `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` ÔÇö nor the OWN label as a conclusion ÔÇö unless\n   `master_paid is False` with a complete reading. When the master's status is `None`, the honest label\n   is an inconclusive one (`DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED_MASTER_UNREADABLE`, or reuse\n   `DD06_INCONCLUSIVE_ÔÇĞ`), with the agent's decrease reported as a fact and no exclusion drawn.\n\n### NIT\n\n- **N-3** *(re-raised, unchanged, demonstrated)* `_resting_oid` (`tools:701-707`) regex-matches any\n  `\"oid\": N`, a `filled` status included, so a filled control order yields\n  `ABORTED_CONTROL_CANCEL_FAILED` with *\"rests on the book ÔÇĞ cancel it by hand\"* ÔÇö the wrong remedy for\n  an open position (`test_DEMO_C`). Findings\n\n### REQUIRED\n\n#### R-1 ÔÇö a partially unreadable wallet is reported as a measured \"did not pay\", and the non-breach exclusion is printed from it\n\n**Where.** `_paid` `tools:355-377` (the `readable` counter at `tools:374` and the\n`return False if readable else None` at `tools:377`); `_attribute` `tools:380-412` (the readings at\n`tools:393`, the own-funds text at `tools:400-406`).\n\n**What happens.** `_balances` reads a wallet through **two different endpoints** and turns a failure of\neither into `None` (`tools:331-336` for `user_state`, `tools:341-345` for `spot_user_state`). `_paid`\ncounts a key as `readable` whenever that one key parses on both sides. So a wallet counts as measured\nas soon as **either** endpoint answers ÔÇö and the verdict `False` is rendered by `_attribute` as the flat\nwords **\"did not pay\"**, with no unreadable marker anywhere in the sentence.\n\n**Why that matters here specifically.** On the account this probe runs against, one of those two keys is\na decoy. The candidate's own comment says so:\n\n> `# A unifiedAccount keeps its USDC in the spot balance (perp accountValue reads 0.0 ÔÇö r1 on testnet);` ÔÇö `tools:486`\n\nEvery dollar the master holds is in `usdc_total`; `accountValue` reads `0.0` before and after, always.\nThat permanently-readable `0.0` is enough to set `readable = 1`. Consequence: **for the master wallet on\nthis account, the `UNREADABLE` path the round-2 repair added can only fire if BOTH endpoints fail at\nonce.** A single transient failure of `spot_user_state` ÔÇö the endpoint that carries the only balance\ncapable of paying ÔÇö is reported as a measurement that it did not make.\n\n**Demonstrated** (`C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH/mutroot3/tests/test_reviewer_partial3.py`, run against the\n**unmodified** candidate, fake venue objects only, `3 passed`):\n\n```\nRESULT : DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED\nFINDING: spotSend was NOT refused and the AGENT wallet's own balance DECREASED while the account's\n         readable balances did not: the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach)\n         [account did not pay, agent wallet paid]\nMASTER READINGS: {'before': {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': '983.987457'},\n                  'after':  {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': None}}\n```\n\nThe record says the account **did not pay**. The account's only funded balance was **never read**. The\nthird probe in that file shows the same output for a run in which the master really did pay 6 USDC ÔÇö the\ndecrease is simply invisible.\n\n**This is deliberate, not an oversight**, which is why it needs a decision rather than a patch: the\nshipped unit test pins the partial case at `tests:677-684` ÔÇö\n\n```python\nassert paid({\"accountValue\": None, \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"},\n            {\"accountValue\": \"5.0\",  \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"}, 1.0) is False\n```\n\nÔÇö under a test named `ÔÇĞ_unreadable_honest`. The parametrized case that does exercise unreadability\n(`tests:595-598`) blanks **both** endpoints, so the partial case has no coverage at all.\n\n**Sub-case R-1(b): the exclusion survives even when the sentence does say UNREADABLE.** With the master\nfully unreadable, the finding still reads\n`ÔÇĞ the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach) [account UNREADABLE, agent wallet paid]`\n(`test_DEMO_A`, ┬ğ4e file). `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` (`tools:403`) is an exclusion claim; it requires a\ncomplete, readable master measurement, and in this branch there is none. The clause\n`\"while the account's readable balances did not\"` (`tools:402-403`) is vacuously true when there are no\nreadable balances, and is being used to carry the conclusion.\n\n**Why REQUIRED and not a NIT.** This is round-2's R-1(b) surviving in the case that matters. Round 2\nasked that an unreadable side be named unreadable and never treated as measured; the repair implemented\nthat only for the all-or-nothing failure, and on this account the all-or-nothing failure is the one shape\nthat essentially cannot occur, because a structurally-zero decoy balance always votes \"readable\". The\nerror direction is the flattering one ÔÇö the tool tells the owner the master did not pay, about money it\ndid not look at ÔÇö and the sentence it prints is this package's deliverable.\n\n**Remedy (small, local, failing inputs already written).** Two lines of intent:\n1. `_paid` should distinguish *measured-and-unchanged* from *not measured*: treat a wallet as measured\n   only when every key parsed on both sides (or, minimally, return `None` when any key was unreadable and\n   no readable key decreased). `tests:677-684` then needs its expectation flipped from `False` to `None`.\n2. `_attribute` must not print `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` ÔÇö nor the OWN label as a conclusion ÔÇö unless\n   `master_paid is False` with a complete reading. When the master's status is `None`, the honest label\n   is an inconclusive one (`DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED_MASTER_UNREADABLE`, or reuse\n   `DD06_INCONCLUSIVE_ÔÇĞ`), with the agent's decrease reported as a fact and no exclusion drawn.\n\n### NIT\n\n- **N-3** *(re-raised, unchanged, demonstrated)* `_resting_oid` (`tools:701-707`) regex-matches any\n  `\"oid\": N`, a `filled` status included, so a filled control order yields\n  `ABORTED_CONTROL_CANCEL_FAILED` with *\"rests on the book ÔÇĞ cancel it by hand\"* ÔÇö the wrong remedy for\n  an open position (`test_DEMO_C`). Require the `resting` key.\n- **N-4** *(re-raised, unchanged, demonstrated)* `_HEX40` (`tools:89`) requires the `0x` prefix, so a bare\n  40-hex address passes both `redact` and the `write_record` guard and is written to the record\n  (`test_DEMO_D`: `\"leak\": \"a1a1ÔÇĞa1\"`). `_HEX64` (`tools:88`) correctly makes the prefix optional.\n  Addresses are public; hygiene only.\n- **N-5** *(re-raised, re-measured)* the worst exact notional over my 2.9 M-mid sweep is\n  `10.499999999999998` at mid 10 416.7 ÔÇö 2e-15 below `CONTROL_MIN_NOTIONAL_USD`, which breaks the suite's\n  own `>=` at `tests:158` for unsampled mids. No venue impact: the floor is $10 and the constant carries\n  $0.50 of margin.\n- **N-6** *(re-raised, reduced)* nothing binds execution to KVM2-P4-03 although packet ┬ğ3 does; the run\n  token is host-agnostic. Fixture tests vs reality ÔÇö what the fixtures still do not carry","+","+| Venue-shaped fact | Could it make the next run misreport? |","+|---|---|","+| **A balance endpoint that fails on one wallet but not the other, or on one of a wallet's two endpoints** | **Yes ÔÇö this is R-1.** `_balances` (`tools:327-352`) calls two different endpoints, `user_state` and `spot_user_state`, and swallows a failure of either into `None`. Findings","+","+### REQUIRED","+","+#### R-1 ÔÇö a partially unreadable wallet is reported as a measured \"did not pay\", and the non-breach exclusion is printed from it","+","+**Where.** `_paid` `tools:355-377` (the `readable` counter at `tools:374` and the","+`return False if readable else None` at `tools:377`); `_attribute` `tools:380-412` (the readings at","+`tools:393`, the own-funds text at `tools:400-406`).","+","+**What happens.** `_balances` reads a wallet through **two different endpoints** and turns a failure of","+either into `None` (`tools:331-336` for `user_state`, `tools:341-345` for `spot_user_state`). `_paid`","+counts a key as `readable` whenever that one key parses on both sides. So a wallet counts as measured","+as soon as **either** endpoint answers ÔÇö and the verdict `False` is rendered by `_attribute` as the flat","+words **\"did not pay\"**, with no unreadable marker anywhere in the sentence.","+","+**Why that matters here specifically.** On the account this probe runs against, one of those two keys is","+a decoy. The candidate's own comment says so:","+","+> `# A unifiedAccount keeps its USDC in the spot balance (perp accountValue reads 0.0 ÔÇö r1 on testnet);` ÔÇö `tools:486`","+","+Every dollar the master holds is in `usdc_total`; `accountValue` reads `0.0` before and after, always.","+That permanently-readable `0.0` is enough to set `readable = 1`. Consequence: **for the master wallet on","+this account, the `UNREADABLE` path the round-2 repair added can only fire if BOTH endpoints fail at","+once.** A single transient failure of `spot_user_state` ÔÇö the endpoint that carries the only balance","+capable of paying ÔÇö is reported as a measurement that it did not make.","+","+**Demonstrated** (`C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH/mutroot3/tests/test_reviewer_partial3.py`, run against the","+**unmodified** candidate, fake venue objects only, `3 passed`):","+","+```","+RESULT : DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED","+FINDING: spotSend was NOT refused and the AGENT wallet's own balance DECREASED while the account's","+         readable balances did not: the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach)","+         [account did not pay, agent wallet paid]","+MASTER READINGS: {'before': {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': '983.987457'},","+                  'after':  {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': None}}","+```","+","+The record says the account **did not pay**. The account's only funded balance was **never read**. The","+third probe in that file shows the same output for a run in which the master really did pay 6 USDC ÔÇö the","+decrease is simply invisible.","+","+**This is deliberate, not an oversight**, which is why it needs a decision rather than a patch: the","+shipped unit test pins the partial case at `tests:677-684` ÔÇö","+","+```python","+assert paid({\"accountValue\": None, \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"},","+            {\"accountValue\": \"5.0\",  \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"}, 1.0) is False","+```","+","+ÔÇö under a test named `ÔÇĞ_unreadable_honest`. The parametrized case that does exercise unreadability","+(`tests:595-598`) blanks **both** endpoints, so the partial case has no coverage at all.","+","+**Sub-case R-1(b): the exclusion survives even when the sentence does say UNREADABLE.** With the master","+fully unreadable, the finding still reads","+`ÔÇĞ the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach) [account UNREADABLE, agent wallet paid]`","+(`test_DEMO_A`, ┬ğ4e file). `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` (`tools:403`) is an exclusion claim; it requires a","+complete, readable master measurement, and in this branch there is none. The clause","+`\"while the account's readable balances did not\"` (`tools:402-403`) is vacuously true when there are no","+readable balances, and is being used to carry the conclusion.","+","+**Why REQUIRED and not a NIT.** This is round-2's R-1(b) surviving in the case that matters. Round 2","+asked that an unreadable side be named unreadable and never treated as measured; the repair implemented","+that only for the all-or-nothing failure, and on this account the all-or-nothing failure is the one shape","+that essentially cannot occur, because a structurally-zero decoy balance always votes \"readable\". That label still stops the run, still exits 2, and its text says \"read the venue response and the ledger before drawing any DD-06 conclusion\" (`tools:407-411`), so no wrong decision is invited ÔåÆ **N-14** |\n| **A funded perp position during the probe window** | Yes, above the threshold. `accountValue` is mark-to-market; a 0.60 USD drift with USDC untouched satisfies the 0.50 threshold of a 1 USDC arm and prints \"DD-06 falsified on testnet\". Findings\n\n### REQUIRED\n\n#### R-1 ÔÇö a partially unreadable wallet is reported as a measured \"did not pay\", and the non-breach exclusion is printed from it\n\n**Where.** `_paid` `tools:355-377` (the `readable` counter at `tools:374` and the\n`return False if readable else None` at `tools:377`); `_attribute` `tools:380-412` (the readings at\n`tools:393`, the own-funds text at `tools:400-406`).\n\n**What happens.** `_balances` reads a wallet through **two different endpoints** and turns a failure of\neither into `None` (`tools:331-336` for `user_state`, `tools:341-345` for `spot_user_state`). `_paid`\ncounts a key as `readable` whenever that one key parses on both sides. So a wallet counts as measured\nas soon as **either** endpoint answers ÔÇö and the verdict `False` is rendered by `_attribute` as the flat\nwords **\"did not pay\"**, with no unreadable marker anywhere in the sentence.\n\n**Why that matters here specifically.** On the account this probe runs against, one of those two keys is\na decoy. The candidate's own comment says so:\n\n> `# A unifiedAccount keeps its USDC in the spot balance (perp accountValue reads 0.0 ÔÇö r1 on testnet);` ÔÇö `tools:486`\n\nEvery dollar the master holds is in `usdc_total`; `accountValue` reads `0.0` before and after, always.\nThat permanently-readable `0.0` is enough to set `readable = 1`. Consequence: **for the master wallet on\nthis account, the `UNREADABLE` path the round-2 repair added can only fire if BOTH endpoints fail at\nonce.** A single transient failure of `spot_user_state` ÔÇö the endpoint that carries the only balance\ncapable of paying ÔÇö is reported as a measurement that it did not make.\n\n**Demonstrated** (`C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH/mutroot3/tests/test_reviewer_partial3.py`, run against the\n**unmodified** candidate, fake venue objects only, `3 passed`):\n\n```\nRESULT : DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED\nFINDING: spotSend was NOT refused and the AGENT wallet's own balance DECREASED while the account's\n         readable balances did not: the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach)\n         [account did not pay, agent wallet paid]\nMASTER READINGS: {'before': {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': '983.987457'},\n                  'after':  {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': None}}\n```\n\nThe record says the account **did not pay**. The account's only funded balance was **never read**. The\nthird probe in that file shows the same output for a run in which the master really did pay 6 USDC ÔÇö the\ndecrease is simply invisible.\n\n**This is deliberate, not an oversight**, which is why it needs a decision rather than a patch: the\nshipped unit test pins the partial case at `tests:677-684` ÔÇö\n\n```python\nassert paid({\"accountValue\": None, \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"},\n            {\"accountValue\": \"5.0\",  \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"}, 1.0) is False\n```\n\nÔÇö under a test named `ÔÇĞ_unreadable_honest`. The parametrized case that does exercise unreadability\n(`tests:595-598`) blanks **both** endpoints, so the partial case has no coverage at all.\n\n**Sub-case R-1(b): the exclusion survives even when the sentence does say UNREADABLE.** With the master\nfully unreadable, the finding still reads\n`ÔÇĞ the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach) [account UNREADABLE, agent wallet paid]`\n(`test_DEMO_A`, ┬ğ4e file). `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` (`tools:403`) is an exclusion claim; it requires a\ncomplete, readable master measurement, and in this branch there is none. The clause\n`\"while the account's readable balances did not\"` (`tools:402-403`) is vacuously true when there are no\nreadable balances, and is being used to carry the conclusion.\n\n**Why REQUIRED and not a NIT.** This is round-2's R-1(b) surviving in the case that matters. Round 2\nasked that an unreadable side be named unreadable and never treated as measured; the repair implemented\nthat only for the all-or-nothing failure, and on this account the all-or-nothing failure is the one shape\nthat essentially cannot occur, because a structurally-zero decoy balance always votes \"readable\". The\nerror direction is the flattering one ÔÇö the tool tells the owner the master did not pay, about money it\ndid not look at ÔÇö and the sentence it prints is this package's deliverable.\n\n**Remedy (small, local, failing inputs already written).** Two lines of intent:\n1. `_paid` should distinguish *measured-and-unchanged* from *not measured*: treat a wallet as measured\n   only when every key parsed on both sides (or, minimally, return `None` when any key was unreadable and\n   no readable key decreased). `tests:677-684` then needs its expectation flipped from `False` to `None`.\n2. `_attribute` must not print `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` ÔÇö nor the OWN label as a conclusion ÔÇö unless\n   `master_paid is False` with a complete reading. When the master's status is `None`, the honest label\n   is an inconclusive one (`DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED_MASTER_UNREADABLE`, or reuse\n   `DD06_INCONCLUSIVE_ÔÇĞ`), with the agent's decrease reported as a fact and no exclusion drawn.\n\n### NIT\n\n- **N-3** *(re-raised, unchanged, demonstrated)* `_resting_oid` (`tools:701-707`) regex-matches any\n  `\"oid\": N`, a `filled` status included, so a filled control order yields\n  `ABORTED_CONTROL_CANCEL_FAILED` with *\"rests on the book ÔÇĞ cancel it by hand\"* ÔÇö the wrong remedy for\n  an open position (`test_DEMO_C`). Findings\n\n### REQUIRED\n\n#### R-1 ÔÇö a partially unreadable wallet is reported as a measured \"did not pay\", and the non-breach exclusion is printed from it\n\n**Where.** `_paid` `tools:355-377` (the `readable` counter at `tools:374` and the\n`return False if readable else None` at `tools:377`); `_attribute` `tools:380-412` (the readings at\n`tools:393`, the own-funds text at `tools:400-406`).\n\n**What happens.** `_balances` reads a wallet through **two different endpoints** and turns a failure of\neither into `None` (`tools:331-336` for `user_state`, `tools:341-345` for `spot_user_state`). `_paid`\ncounts a key as `readable` whenever that one key parses on both sides. So a wallet counts as measured\nas soon as **either** endpoint answers ÔÇö and the verdict `False` is rendered by `_attribute` as the flat\nwords **\"did not pay\"**, with no unreadable marker anywhere in the sentence.\n\n**Why that matters here specifically.** On the account this probe runs against, one of those two keys is\na decoy. The candidate's own comment says so:\n\n> `# A unifiedAccount keeps its USDC in the spot balance (perp accountValue reads 0.0 ÔÇö r1 on testnet);` ÔÇö `tools:486`\n\nEvery dollar the master holds is in `usdc_total`; `accountValue` reads `0.0` before and after, always.\nThat permanently-readable `0.0` is enough to set `readable = 1`. Consequence: **for the master wallet on\nthis account, the `UNREADABLE` path the round-2 repair added can only fire if BOTH endpoints fail at\nonce.** A single transient failure of `spot_user_state` ÔÇö the endpoint that carries the only balance\ncapable of paying ÔÇö is reported as a measurement that it did not make.\n\n**Demonstrated** (`C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH/mutroot3/tests/test_reviewer_partial3.py`, run against the\n**unmodified** candidate, fake venue objects only, `3 passed`):\n\n```\nRESULT : DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED\nFINDING: spotSend was NOT refused and the AGENT wallet's own balance DECREASED while the account's\n         readable balances did not: the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach)\n         [account did not pay, agent wallet paid]\nMASTER READINGS: {'before': {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': '983.987457'},\n                  'after':  {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': None}}\n```\n\nThe record says the account **did not pay**. The account's only funded balance was **never read**. The\nthird probe in that file shows the same output for a run in which the master really did pay 6 USDC ÔÇö the\ndecrease is simply invisible.\n\n**This is deliberate, not an oversight**, which is why it needs a decision rather than a patch: the\nshipped unit test pins the partial case at `tests:677-684` ÔÇö\n\n```python\nassert paid({\"accountValue\": None, \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"},\n            {\"accountValue\": \"5.0\",  \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"}, 1.0) is False\n```\n\nÔÇö under a test named `ÔÇĞ_unreadable_honest`. The parametrized case that does exercise unreadability\n(`tests:595-598`) blanks **both** endpoints, so the partial case has no coverage at all.\n\n**Sub-case R-1(b): the exclusion survives even when the sentence does say UNREADABLE.** With the master\nfully unreadable, the finding still reads\n`ÔÇĞ the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach) [account UNREADABLE, agent wallet paid]`\n(`test_DEMO_A`, ┬ğ4e file). `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` (`tools:403`) is an exclusion claim; it requires a\ncomplete, readable master measurement, and in this branch there is none. The clause\n`\"while the account's readable balances did not\"` (`tools:402-403`) is vacuously true when there are no\nreadable balances, and is being used to carry the conclusion.\n\n**Why REQUIRED and not a NIT.** This is round-2's R-1(b) surviving in the case that matters. Round 2\nasked that an unreadable side be named unreadable and never treated as measured; the repair implemented\nthat only for the all-or-nothing failure, and on this account the all-or-nothing failure is the one shape\nthat essentially cannot occur, because a structurally-zero decoy balance always votes \"readable\". The\nerror direction is the flattering one ÔÇö the tool tells the owner the master did not pay, about money it\ndid not look at ÔÇö and the sentence it prints is this package's deliverable.\n\n**Remedy (small, local, failing inputs already written).** Two lines of intent:\n1. `_paid` should distinguish *measured-and-unchanged* from *not measured*: treat a wallet as measured\n   only when every key parsed on both sides (or, minimally, return `None` when any key was unreadable and\n   no readable key decreased). `tests:677-684` then needs its expectation flipped from `False` to `None`.\n2. `_attribute` must not print `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` ÔÇö nor the OWN label as a conclusion ÔÇö unless\n   `master_paid is False` with a complete reading. When the master's status is `None`, the honest label\n   is an inconclusive one (`DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED_MASTER_UNREADABLE`, or reuse\n   `DD06_INCONCLUSIVE_ÔÇĞ`), with the agent's decrease reported as a fact and no exclusion drawn.\n\n### NIT\n\n- **N-3** *(re-raised, unchanged, demonstrated)* `_resting_oid` (`tools:701-707`) regex-matches any\n  `\"oid\": N`, a `filled` status included, so a filled control order yields\n  `ABORTED_CONTROL_CANCEL_FAILED` with *\"rests on the book ÔÇĞ cancel it by hand\"* ÔÇö the wrong remedy for\n  an open position (`test_DEMO_C`). Findings\n\n### REQUIRED\n\n#### R-1 ÔÇö a partially unreadable wallet is reported as a measured \"did not pay\", and the non-breach exclusion is printed from it\n\n**Where.** `_paid` `tools:355-377` (the `readable` counter at `tools:374` and the\n`return False if readable else None` at `tools:377`); `_attribute` `tools:380-412` (the readings at\n`tools:393`, the own-funds text at `tools:400-406`).\n\n**What happens.** `_balances` reads a wallet through **two different endpoints** and turns a failure of\neither into `None` (`tools:331-336` for `user_state`, `tools:341-346` for `spot_user_state`). `_paid`\ncounts a key as `readable` whenever that one key parses on both sides. So a wallet counts as measured\nas soon as **either** endpoint answers ÔÇö and the verdict `False` is rendered by `_attribute` as the flat\nwords **\"did not pay\"**, with no unreadable marker anywhere in the sentence.\n\n**Why that matters here specifically.** On the account this probe runs against, one of those two keys is\na decoy. The candidate's own comment says so:\n\n> `# A unifiedAccount keeps its USDC in the spot balance (perp accountValue reads 0.0 ÔÇö r1 on testnet);` ÔÇö `tools:486`\n\nEvery dollar the master holds is in `usdc_total`; `accountValue` reads `0.0` before and after, always.\nThat permanently-readable `0.0` is enough to set `readable = 1`. Consequence: **for the master wallet on\nthis account, the `UNREADABLE` path the round-2 repair added can only fire if BOTH endpoints fail at\nonce.** A single transient failure of `spot_user_state` ÔÇö the endpoint that carries the only balance\ncapable of paying ÔÇö is reported as a measurement that it did not make.\n\n**Demonstrated** (`C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH/mutroot3/tests/test_reviewer_partial3.py`, run against the\n**unmodified** candidate, fake venue objects only, `3 passed`):\n\n```\nRESULT : DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED\nFINDING: spotSend was NOT refused and the AGENT wallet's own balance DECREASED while the account's\n         readable balances did not: the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach)\n         [account did not pay, agent wallet paid]\nMASTER READINGS: {'before': {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': '983.987457'},\n                  'after':  {'accountValue': '0.0', 'usdc_total': None}}\n```\n\nThe record says the account **did not pay**. The account's only funded balance was **never read**. The\nthird probe in that file shows the same output for a run in which the master really did pay 6 USDC ÔÇö the\ndecrease is simply invisible.\n\n**This is deliberate, not an oversight**, which is why it needs a decision rather than a patch: the\nshipped unit test pins the partial case at `tests:677-684` ÔÇö\n\n```python\nassert paid({\"accountValue\": None, \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"},\n            {\"accountValue\": \"5.0\",  \"usdc_total\": \"10.0\"}, 1.0) is False\n```\n\nÔÇö under a test named `ÔÇĞ_unreadable_honest`. The parametrized case that does exercise unreadability\n(`tests:595-598`) blanks **both** endpoints, so the partial case has no coverage at all.\n\n**Sub-case R-1(b): the exclusion survives even when the sentence does say UNREADABLE.** With the master\nfully unreadable, the finding still reads\n`ÔÇĞ the agent moved its own funds (r3 shape; not the DD-06 breach) [account UNREADABLE, agent wallet paid]`\n(`test_DEMO_A`, ┬ğ4e file). `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` (`tools:403`) is an exclusion claim; it requires a\ncomplete, readable master measurement, and in this branch there is none. The clause\n`\"while the account's readable balances did not\"` (`tools:402-403`) is vacuously true when there are no\nreadable balances, and is being used to carry the conclusion.\n\n**Why REQUIRED and not a NIT.** This is round-2's R-1(b) surviving in the case that matters. Round 2\nasked that an unreadable side be named unreadable and never treated as measured; the repair implemented\nthat only for the all-or-nothing failure, and on this account the all-or-nothing failure is the one shape\nthat essentially cannot occur, because a structurally-zero decoy balance always votes \"readable\". The\nerror direction is the flattering one ÔÇö the tool tells the owner the master did not pay, about money it\ndid not look at ÔÇö and the sentence it prints is this package's deliverable.\n\n**Remedy (small, local, failing inputs already written).** Two lines of intent:\n1. `_paid` should distinguish *measured-and-unchanged* from *not measured*: treat a wallet as measured\n   only when every key parsed on both sides (or, minimally, return `None` when any key was unreadable and\n   no readable key decreased). `tests:677-684` then needs its expectation flipped from `False` to `None`.\n2. `_attribute` must not print `\"not the DD-06 breach\"` ÔÇö nor the OWN label as a conclusion ÔÇö unless\n   `master_paid is False` with a complete reading. When the master's status is `None`, the honest label\n   is an inconclusive one (`DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED_MASTER_UNREADABLE`, or reuse\n   `DD06_INCONCLUSIVE_ÔÇĞ`), with the agent's decrease reported as a fact and no exclusion drawn.\n\n### NIT\n\n- **N-3** *(re-raised, unchanged, demonstrated)* `_resting_oid` (`tools:701-707`) regex-matches any\n  `\"oid\": N`, a `filled` status included, so a filled control order yields\n  `ABORTED_CONTROL_CANCEL_FAILED` with *\"rests on the book ÔÇĞ cancel it by hand\"* ÔÇö the wrong remedy for\n  an open position (`test_DEMO_C`).
